Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated substantial promise in spatial understanding. Existing works typically incorporate prior knowledge extracted from a pre-trained foundation model to further enhance the spatial awareness of MLLMs. In this paper, we first reveal that when integrating diverse foundation models into MLLMs, different models provide complementary spatial priors that benefit different tasks. Motivated by this, we propose $\textbf{ViPS}$, a novel multi-model prior framework designed to fully unleash the potential of incorporating multiple $\textbf{Vi}$sual $\textbf{P}$riors from diverse models into MLLMs for $\textbf{S}$patial understanding. Specifically, ViPS introduces an Efficient Prior Proxy to generate multiple foundational priors with minimal inference overhead, and a Dynamic Prior Fusion mechanism to achieve harmonious and context-aware prior fusion and injection from the prior proxies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ViPS successfully harmonizes diverse visual priors, establishing new state-of-the-art performance across multiple complex spatial reasoning and 3D spatial understanding benchmarks. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/vips

</details>


### [75] [SUFLECA: Scaling Up Feature Learning for CAD-to-image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15058)
*Saad Ejaz,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Javier Civera,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SUFLECA是一种弱监督的零样本CAD对齐框架，通过扩展几何特征学习与几何一致性匹配，在ScanNet25k上以更低计算量超越现有零样本和全监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本CAD对齐方法依赖外观驱动的特征匹配，在遮挡或仿真到真实域迁移场景下性能下降严重。

Method: 1) 利用归一化物体坐标(NOCs)监督在674K图像上学习几何感知特征；2) 提出几何一致性匹配算法建立可靠的CAD到图像的一对一对应关系。

Result: 在ScanNet25k上达到33.4%/42.3%的类别/实例准确率，超越最强零样本基线10.3/12.2个百分点，且首次在该基准上超过全监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过扩大几何特征学习规模和一致性匹配，无需迭代位姿优化即可实现亚秒级精确对齐，证明了弱监督几何学习在CAD对齐中的有效性。

Abstract: CAD-to-image alignment aims to estimate an object's 9D pose (rotation, translation, and anisotropic scale) from a single RGB image, enabling applications in robotics and augmented reality. Recent zero-shot methods use visual foundation models to match image regions to CAD models, yet typically their correspondences are appearance-driven and degrade under occlusion or sim-to-real domain shift. To address these limitations, we introduce SUFLECA (Scaling Up Feature LEarning for CAD Alignment), a weakly-supervised framework for zero-shot CAD alignment with two key contributions. First, SUFLECA scales up geometry-grounded feature learning from pretrained visual representations through Normalized Object Coordinates (NOCs) supervision on 674K images spanning 12 real and synthetic datasets, learning compact geometry-aware features that generalize across domains. Second, we propose a geometrically consistent matching algorithm that establishes reliable one-to-one CAD-to-image correspondences. Together, these contributions enable accurate, sub-second alignment per object instance without iterative pose refinement. On ScanNet25k, SUFLECA achieves 33.4%/42.3% category/instance accuracy, outperforming, with a smaller computational footprint, the strongest zero-shot baseline by 10.3/12.2 percentage points and, for the first time on this benchmark, even surpassing fully supervised methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/snt-arg/SUFLECA

</details>


### [76] [Towards Hierarchical Structure Understanding of Newspaper Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15082)
*William Mocaër,Solène Tarride,Thomas Constum,Merveilles Agbeti-Messan,Tom Simon,Clément Chatelain,Stéphane Nicolas,Pierrick Tranouez,Sébastien Cretin,Thierry Paquet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种报纸结构理解方法：模块化流水线（YOLO+LayoutReader+自定义分割算法）和端到端Transformer架构Tiramisu，并发布了用于评估历史报纸层次信息检索的新数据集Finlam La Liberté。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报纸图像结构复杂（嵌套层次、密集异构布局），现有方法难以有效理解其层次结构，需要更灵活的模块化方案和端到端建模方案。

Method: 1）模块化流水线：YOLO进行布局检测，LayoutReader预测阅读顺序，自定义算法进行文章分割；2）Tiramisu：基于迭代分层Transformer的端到端架构，通过并行注意力机制同时完成章节与文章分离、区块定位、语义分类和阅读顺序预测。

Result: 两种方法均能有效重建复杂的报纸层次结构，对比分析展示了各自在大规模文档数字化中的优势。

Conclusion: 模块化流水线灵活可解释，Tiramisu端到端建模能力强，两者互补；代码和合成数据生成器已开源。

Abstract: Understanding newspaper images remains a challenging task due to their complex, nested hierarchical structures and dense, heterogeneous layouts. In this paper, we explore two complementary approaches for newspaper structure understanding. First, we present a modular bottom-up pipeline that combines state-of-the-art open-source models: YOLO for layout detection, LayoutReader for reading order prediction, and a custom algorithm for article segmentation. This approach leverages existing robust components while maintaining flexibility and interpretability. Second, we introduce Tiramisu (Tiered Transformers for Hierarchical Structure Understanding), a novel end-to-end transformer-based architecture that explicitly models document hierarchy through an iterative tiered process. Tiramisu performs section and article separation, block localization, semantic categorization, and reading order prediction using highly parallelized attention mechanisms. Finally, we release Finlam La Liberté, a new dataset designed specifically for evaluating hierarchical information retrieval in historical newspapers. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of both approaches in reconstructing complex newspaper hierarchies, with comparative analysis highlighting their respective strengths for scalable document digitization. The Tiramisu training code, including the synthetic newspaper generator, is available at https://git.litislab.fr/tiramisu/tiramisu-newspaper-articles-extractor.

</details>


### [77] [Quantifying Training Membership Information in the Hyperspherical Embedding Geometry of Face Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15084)
*Ünsal Öztürk,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了人脸识别模型中成员与非成员嵌入向量在几何特性上的差异，发现训练身份数量是影响这种可分离性的最关键因素，而骨干网络和损失函数影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于角度间隔损失的人脸识别模型在训练时仅作用于训练身份，导致非成员身份可能形成不同的几何聚类特性，作者希望量化这种差异及其受训练时因素影响的程度。

Method: 在IResNet骨干网络大小、损失函数类型、训练时长和训练身份数量四个维度上进行因子实验，横跨180个人脸识别模型，计算四个基于聚类几何的统计量，并在9个基准上进行评估。

Result: 训练身份数量对成员/非成员可分离性影响最大，骨干网络和损失函数贡献较小；在同一域内，几何成员信号随训练身份增加而单调递减；跨域（姿态、年龄、质量、种族）非成员基准会放大表面的成员信号。

Conclusion: 通过融合所有四个统计量并使用学习分类器，可以揭示超越最佳单一统计量的额外成员信息。

Abstract: Face recognition models represent each face as an embedding vector on the unit hypersphere by clustering embeddings of the same identity while pushing different identities apart through angular-margin losses. Because these losses act only on training identities, non-member identities may form clusters with different geometric properties. In this paper, we quantify the magnitude of this difference and what training-time factors control it. We compute four statistics based on cluster geometry across 180 face recognition models in a factorial design over IResNet backbone size, loss head, training duration, and the number of training identities, and evaluate each configuration on nine benchmarks. Our results indicate that the number of training identities has the largest effect on member/non-member separability, while backbone and loss head contribute far less, and that, on a same-domain held-out reference, the geometric membership signal decreases monotonically as more identities are added to training. We provide an analysis of cross-domain (pose, age, quality, ethnicity) non-member benchmarks and report that these inflate the apparent membership signal. Finally, we fuse all four statistics with a learned classifier to reveal additional membership information beyond the best individual statistic.

</details>


### [78] [AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15094)
*Sarthak Jain,Qiran Hu,Zhen Zhu,Yaoyao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AlphaWiSE方法，通过后验权重空间插值组合两个冻结的检查点，为每个参数张量拟合一个标量插值系数，在持续学习多模态检索中优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型（如CLIP）在持续适应新数据时，会破坏早期阶段学到的跨模态对齐能力，而传统持续学习方法仅返回单个检查点，无法在不同检索方向上平衡稳定性-可塑性。

Method: 提出AlphaWiSE后验权重空间插值方法，组合两个冻结的源检查点，为每个对齐的参数张量（由检查点键标识）拟合一个标量插值系数（共享于所有张量条目），系数在小样本记忆上拟合，生成一个插值后的检查点。

Result: 在音频-图像-文本检索任务上，该方法在多个检索方向和评估指标上持续优于强持续学习基线。

Conclusion: AlphaWiSE部署后与源检查点具有相同架构和参数量，不增加推理时间，有效缓解了多模态持续学习中的跨模态对齐灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [79] [QuReC: All-in-One Image Restoration with Query-Specific Guidance and Local-Global Response Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15097)
*Shen Zhou,Jinghui Zhang,Wenbo Huang,Xuwei Qian,Zhen Wu,Guangwen Peng,Zhiyuan Li,Ding Ding,Dian Shen,Fang Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QuReC统一框架，通过退化引导查询重建和局部-全局响应校准，实现多退化类型的全场景图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全场景图像恢复方法依赖图像级提示或共享引导，无法处理空间异质或混合退化，且缺乏可靠的局部与全局信息聚合机制。

Method: QuReC包含两个核心模块：1) 退化引导查询重建模块(DQRM)，通过将空间查询与退化原型空间匹配，重建查询特定的退化感知表征，并引入弱监督原型匹配学习策略；2) 局部-全局响应校准模块(LGRCM)，进行局部-全局双分支聚合并用可学习先验校准响应。

Result: 在多个全场景图像恢复基准上取得了优越的性能。

Conclusion: QuReC通过细粒度空间自适应引导和可靠的局部-全局特征聚合，有效解决了空间异质和混合退化问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images degraded by multiple corruption types using a single unified model. Existing methods typically rely on image-level prompts or shared guidance to handle diverse degradations. However, such a paradigm becomes inadequate when degradations are spatially heterogeneous or even coexist in mixed forms within a single image. Yet spatially adaptive guidance alone is not sufficient, since accurate restoration also requires each spatial query to reliably aggregate complementary information from local neighborhoods and global contexts. To this end, we propose QuReC, a unified framework for all-in-one image restoration. QuReC consists of a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM). Specifically, DQRM matches each spatial query against a degradation prototype space to reconstruct a query-specific degradation-aware representation, thereby providing fine-grained spatially adaptive restoration guidance. To further stabilize this query-wise matching process, we introduce a weakly supervised prototype matching learning strategy to improve optimization stability and degradation semantic consistency. Meanwhile, LGRCM performs local-global dual-branch aggregation and calibrates the aggregated responses with learnable priors, improving the reliability of feature aggregation and the coordination between local detail modeling and global context modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that QuReC achieves superior performance on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks. The code is released at https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC.

</details>


### [80] [DAPGNet: Dynamic Adaptive Physics-Guided Graph Diffusion Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15128)
*Pengkun Wang,Weijia Cao,Ning Wang,Xiaofei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAPGNet是一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，通过将光谱物理先验注入图学习过程，在高光谱图像分类任务上显著优于现有各类基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的高光谱图像分类方法通常依赖空间邻近性、超像素连通性等构建图拓扑，忽略了连续波段所携带的光谱物理先验信息，导致该先验对图结构估计和消息传播的影响有限。

Method: 首先将连续光谱响应编码为节点级多尺度物理先验表示；然后结合光谱-空间亲和度、物理先验一致性和空间距离构建物理先验感知的稀疏图拓扑；在图扩散过程中将边权重转化为加性注意力偏置，并通过物理门控机制对图聚合特征与物理先验投影特征进行插值融合；最后结合交叉尺度融合、主分类损失、辅助监督和二阶光谱平滑正则化进行优化。

Result: 在Indian Pines、WHU-Hi-LongKou、Houston2013和Houston2018四个数据集上，DAPGNet在OA、AA和Kappa指标上均优于CNN、Transformer、Mamba和图网络等基线方法，AA值超出最强竞争方法3.64~7.31个百分点。

Conclusion: 消融和敏感性分析验证了物理先验提取、先验感知拓扑构建、物理门控传播和光谱平滑正则化之间的互补效应，证明了将光谱物理先验注入关系级图学习的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires reliable pixel-relation modeling under spectral variability, mixed pixels, and heterogeneous boundaries. Existing graph-based HSI classifiers usually construct graph topology from spatial proximity, superpixel connectivity, or learned feature affinity. However, the spectral physical prior carried by contiguous bands has limited influence on topology estimation and message propagation. This paper presents DAPGNet, a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that injects a structure-constrained physical prior into relation-level graph learning. DAPGNet first encodes contiguous spectral responses into node-wise multiscale physical-prior representations. A two-stage graph constructor then combines spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance to form a physical-prior-aware sparse topology. During graph diffusion, learned edge weights are transformed into additive attention biases, while a physical gate performs node-wise and feature-wise interpolation between graph-aggregated features and projected physical-prior features. Cross-scale fusion integrates node states from different diffusion depths, and the network is optimized with main classification, auxiliary supervision, and second-order spectral smoothness regularization. Experiments on Indian Pines, WHU-Hi-LongKou, Houston2013, and Houston2018 show that DAPGNet achieves the best OA, AA, and Kappa among representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines. It improves AA over the strongest competing method by 3.64 to 7.31 percentage points across the four datasets. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further support the complementary effects of physical-prior extraction, prior-aware topology construction, physics-gated propagation, and spectral smoothness regularization.

</details>


### [81] [Ray-based phase error correction for miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP under single-directional hyperbolic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15139)
*Seung-Jae Son,Yatong An,Jae-Sang Hyun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于射线的相位误差校正框架，用于解决微型DOE投影仪FPP系统中的非线性投影伪影问题，通过单方向双曲条纹图案估计投影针孔，无需立体标定，仅需单个标定位姿即可构建精化模型，显著提升非线性投影条件下的重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型DOE投影仪因非线性投影特性和有限的图案可控性，导致严重的相位伪影，影响FPP系统的三维重建精度。

Method: 提出基于射线的相位误差校正框架，沿投影光线建模相位伪影，融入投影仪几何信息；通过单方向双曲条纹图案估计投影针孔位置，无需立体标定；采用数据高效策略，仅需单个标定位姿即可构建精化模型。

Result: 在微型DOE投影仪FPP系统上实验表明，该方法在非线性投影条件下显著提升了重建精度，验证了鲁棒性和物理一致性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效校正了微型投影仪的相位伪影，具有物理一致性和数据高效性，适用于非线性投影场景。

Abstract: Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) systems using miniaturized DOE pro-jectors often suffer from severe phase artifacts due to nonlinear projection characteristics and limited pattern controllability. We propose a ray-based phase error correction framework that models phase artifacts along projection rays from the projector pinhole, incorporating projector geometry without re-lying on image-domain processing or neighboring pixels. A projector pinhole estimation method based on a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern is introduced, through which projector geometry can be recovered without stereo calibration. In addition, a data-efficient strategy constructs the re-finement model from a single calibration pose. Experiments on miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection conditions, confirming the robustness and physical consistency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [82] [MAGiSt3R: Multi-Agent Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15211)
*Ziren Gong,Xiaohan Li,Fabio Tosi,Ninghui Xu,Stefano Mattoccia,Jianfei Cai,Matteo Poggi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAGiSt3R是一个多智能体3D重建框架，能从单目RGB视频中同时进行相机跟踪和3D重建，速度达到近10 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在速度、精度或处理长视频时存在相机漂移问题，需更高效且准确的解决方案。

Method: 利用3R系列的前馈模型处理RGB视频并回归局部点云图，通过MAGMA合并模型在智能体内部和智能体之间合并局部地图，并采用位姿图优化缓解相机漂移。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，MAGiSt3R在重建精度和相机跟踪精度上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: MAGiSt3R通过多智能体协作和前馈机制实现了高效、高精度的3D重建与相机跟踪，为实时单目视频3D重建提供了新方案。

Abstract: This paper presents MAGiSt3R, a multi-agent 3D reconstruction framework performing reconstruction and camera tracking for monocular RGB videos at almost 10 FPS. MAGiSt3R relies on a feed-forward model from the 3R family to process RGB videos and regress local point maps, and on a merging model, MAGMA, that combines local maps at both intra-agent and inter-agent levels to obtain the final global point map. Furthermore, MAGiSt3R performs pose graph optimization to mitigate cumulative camera drift occurring along the feed-forward pipeline. We evaluate MAGiSt3R on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [83] [Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15216)
*Maya Varma,Jean-Benoit Delbrouck,Sophie Ostmeier,Akshay Chaudhari,Curtis Langlotz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出系统性错位检测任务，用于识别多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在生成图像描述时的重复性错误。作者提出Symbal方法（双阶段结构+基础模型）和SymbalBench基准（含170万图像-文本对），在63.8%的数据集上正确识别系统性错位，相比基线提升近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在生成图像描述时常引入错误，导致图文对错位。现有工作缺少对这类重复性、与特定视觉特征相关的系统性错位的检测方法。

Method: 提出Symbal，采用双阶段结构，利用现成的基础模型（off-the-shelf foundation models）识别系统性错位，并以自然语言总结结果。同时构建SymbalBench基准，涵盖自然图像和医学图像两个领域，含170万图文对、420个视觉语言数据集及标注的系统性错位。

Result: Symbal在SymbalBench上正确识别63.8%数据集的系统性错位，领先最接近的基线近4倍。真实场景评估表明，(1) Symbal能准确发现四种MLLM生成描述中的系统性错位，(2) 是审计现成图像描述数据集的有效工具。

Conclusion: 提出的新任务、方法和基准有助于用户审计MLLM生成的描述并识别关键错误，无需访问底层MLLM模型。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.

</details>


### [84] [Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15220)
*Moyao Tian,Shijia Liu,Yan Yang,Xin Yuan,Minshi Chen,Wei Wang,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构-语义互学习框架(SSRL)，通过细粒度结构解耦和闭环语义校准，将无监督可见光-红外行人重识别的开环关联转变为自纠正闭环系统，在SYSU-MM01和RegDB上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督可见光-红外行人重识别方法采用渐进式关联范式，但存在两个关键瓶颈：依赖模糊的全局表示，以及伪标签噪声在开环方式下 unchecked 传播，导致跨模态识别效果受限。

Method: 提出SSRL框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 细粒度结构解耦(FSD)，提取判别性身体部位基元作为可靠的空间锚点；2) 闭环语义校准(CSC)，在每个epoch重建共享语义原型并反馈回训练循环，在下一聚类周期前过滤伪标签噪声。结构学习与语义学习相互促进。

Result: 在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上超越现有无监督方法，并在RegDB上超越多个有监督方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构-语义互学习，将开环关联转变为自纠正闭环系统，有效缓解了模态差异和伪标签噪声问题，实现了鲁棒的跨模态表示学习。

Abstract: Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.

</details>


### [85] [Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15227)
*Mohammed Amine Kerkouri,Daphné Senggaran,Renaud Jusiak,Océane Lehmann,Marouane Tliba,Claire Wardak,Emmanuelle Houy-Durand,Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer,Aladine Chetouani,Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过比较自闭症成人、专业艺术家和神经典型者在观看30幅画作时的眼动数据，发现自闭症成人的视觉探索在空间上与艺术家相似（广泛探索），但在时间上具有独特特征（注视更短、驻留更少、扫描路径最不一致），形成了独特的群体注意模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往自闭症眼动研究多使用社交场景而非艺术作品，且只分析注视位置而忽略时间顺序；本研究旨在系统比较自闭症成人、艺术家和神经典型者在自由观看艺术作品时的空间和时间眼动模式差异。

Method: 三组被试（自闭症成人、专业艺术家、神经典型者）分别观看30幅画作15秒；空间维度使用6种显著性指标比较注视密度图；时间维度使用MultiMatch、ScanMatch、FDISS和DTW比较扫描路径；注视点统一使用离散阈值算法提取。

Result: 艺术家与神经典型者在空间（密度图相关CC=0.96）和时间上几乎无法区分；自闭症组空间探索范围与艺术家相似，但时间特征独特（注视更短、驻留更少、扫描路径最不一致）；自闭症组在任何指标上都不特别接近艺术家，反而略微更接近神经典型者。

Conclusion: 自闭症成人观看艺术是一种独特的群体特异性注意模式，体现在空间和时间两个维度，支持开发群体条件化的审美注意模型。

Abstract: How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.

</details>


### [86] [CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15231)
*Yizhou Fang,Pujin Cheng,Yixiang Liu,Xiaoying Tang,Longxi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CRISP是一种无需目标域数据、无需测试时参数更新的源域无监督分割鲁棒性框架，通过利用概率排名的稳定性在分布偏移下生成鲁棒的 spatial 先验，显著提升医学图像分割的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中的分布偏移是临床转化的核心瓶颈，现有域适应方法受限于预定义偏移或伪标签，无法应对真实世界中无限且不可预测的分布变化。

Method: 提出"正区域排名稳定性"假设，通过潜在特征扰动导出高精度（HP）核心与高召回率（HR）支撑区域，再通过迭代训练框架渐进压缩HP和HR得到最终分割。

Result: 在多中心心脏MRI和肺血管CT分割任务上，CRISP在HD95指标上分别降低了0.14（提升7.0%）、1.90（提升13.1%）和8.39（提升38.9%）像素，显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CRISP无需目标域数据或测试时自适应，仅凭源域冻结权重即可在未知分布偏移下实现鲁棒分割，为医学影像域泛化提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.

</details>


### [87] [ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15246)
*Christos Korgialas,Gabriel Lee Jun Rong,Dion Jia Xu Ho,Pai Chet Ng,Xiaoxiao Miao,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARMOR++ 是一个利用视觉语言模型和大语言模型的多智能体框架，通过五种互补扰动原语和智能编排，显著提升了深度伪造检测器在黑盒无查询攻击下的迁移成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在面临黑盒对抗迁移时可靠性下降，因为检测器依赖易受攻击的架构特定特征；同时现有迁移攻击缺乏语义感知能力，在无查询约束下从CNN到Transformer的迁移效果不佳。

Method: 框架使用Qwen2.5-VL视觉语言模型提供空间语义先验，Qwen3大语言模型负责原语选择、自适应超参数重参数化和熵正则化扰动混合，集成了五种互补扰动原语（密集优化、显著性方法、空间变换、频域扰动、块结构修改）。

Result: 在AADD-2025基准上，ARMOR++在低质量和高质量图像场景下均显著优于现有基线，盲目标攻击成功率（ASR）较最先进的智能体基线有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 当前深度伪造检测器部署存在显著的可靠性差距，而智能体化编排能够有效识别潜在漏洞，提升攻击迁移性。

Abstract: The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [88] [HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15255)
*Pengcheng Zhou,Xuanyu Liu,Yanchen Yin,Bobo Li,Shengqiong Wu,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为HoloGeo的证据驱动推理框架，用于缓解视觉语言模型在地理定位中的地标偏差问题，并构建了LandmarkBias-3K基准和BF-30k数据集，实验表明该方法在保持原有性能的同时显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在地理定位中容易受到地标偏差影响，即过度依赖地标特征而忽略其他地理线索，导致定位不准确。作者希望系统性地研究这一问题并提出缓解方法。

Method: 首先设计了偏差强度(BI)和偏差危害性(BH)两个量化指标，构建了LandmarkBias-3K基准数据集。然后提出HoloGeo框架，基于BF-30k数据集（包含结构化多证据无偏差推理链），通过多维奖励机制鼓励模型均衡关注多种视觉线索，实现证据驱动的联合推理。

Result: HoloGeo在IM2GPS3K和YFCC4k上保持了优异的性能，同时在LandmarkBias-3K上显著优于现有的开源视觉语言模型，验证了其在鲁棒地理空间推理上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地揭示了地标偏差问题，并提供了有效的缓解方案，为提升视觉语言模型在地理定位任务中的可靠性和鲁棒性做出了贡献。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.

</details>


### [89] [Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15268)
*Guang Yang,Wentian Xu,Siyu Wang,Betty Raman,Lei Li,Vicente Grau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MCF-Net，一种融合心脏运动信息与超声基础模型的多视角融合框架，用于心肌梗死的节段定位。仅需极稀疏的标注（单个模板帧），在节段级MI定位上达到72.4% F1和84.9%准确率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心肌运动分析方法要么依赖人工设计的特征或密集监督（标注成本高），要么基于基础模型但仅用单视角且节段定位不可靠，尤其在心尖视图下存在视角依赖性模糊问题。

Method: 1) 使用预训练的EchoPrime基础模型提取双视角视觉特征；2) 通过单张标注模板帧转移至视频进行点追踪，实现极稀疏监督的心脏运动建模；3) 运动导出的节段感知软掩码提供粗略空间先验，增强困难心肌节段特征；4) 运动条件融合机制跨视角整合运动与视觉信息。

Result: 在节段级MI定位上，MCF-Net达到72.4% F1和84.9%准确率，优于现有的纯运动、纯视觉及融合基线方法。

Conclusion: MCF-Net通过运动引导的多视角融合，有效解决了单视角下节段定位不可靠的问题，且仅需极稀疏标注，在MI定位任务上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.

</details>


### [90] [Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15271)
*Baback Elmieh,Lynn Tsai,Zeman Li,Srinivas Kaza,Tiancheng Sun,Gabor Csapo,Ali Behrouz,Yuan Deng,Stephen Lombardi,Steven M. Seitz,Xuan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦记忆更新与记忆应用频率的在线新视角合成方法，通过周期性记忆更新、逐帧记忆应用、交叉视图注意力以及辅助记忆损失和记忆缓存策略，在动态场景中实现了实时、最先进性能，并支持分钟级在线记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线新视角合成需要在实时约束下维持长期记忆以重建临时遮挡区域，但现有TTT模型每帧进行梯度记忆更新，计算成本高且长上下文中不稳定，难以满足实时应用需求。

Method: 将记忆更新与记忆应用解耦，采用周期性记忆更新+逐帧记忆应用；使用交叉视图注意力处理记忆状态与当前帧之间的形变；引入辅助记忆损失强制场景内化；提出记忆缓存策略防止权重灾难性漂移。

Result: 在动态人体运动场景上实现了实时、最先进的性能，并支持分钟级在线记忆。

Conclusion: 通过解耦记忆更新与应用频率，结合辅助记忆损失和记忆缓存策略，可以在满足实时约束的同时实现长期稳定的动态场景记忆，验证了该方法在在线新视角合成中的有效性。

Abstract: Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

</details>


### [91] [MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15273)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Jun Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MeanFlowNFT，将强化学习（RL）成功应用于MeanFlow生成器，通过构建瞬时速度预测器来桥接平均速度与瞬时速度，使得DiffusionNFT的RL优化框架得以适用，同时保持MeanFlow的快速少步采样优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MeanFlow生成器具有快速少步采样的优势，但现有的强化学习方法（如DiffusionNFT）针对的是瞬时速度模型，无法直接应用于基于平均速度的MeanFlow。作者旨在填补这一空白，将RL对齐能力扩展到MeanFlow上。

Method: 受MeanFlow恒等式启发，构建一个诱导的瞬时速度预测器，将DiffusionNFT目标应用于该预测器进行奖励优化，实际采样时仍使用平均速度以保持快速生成能力。

Result: 在图像和视频生成实验中，MeanFlowNFT一致性地改进了基线，在SD3.5-M上8个指标中6个超越先前最先进的RL调优少步生成器，且在Wan 2.1上4步采样达到84.33 VBench分数，超越50步的LongCat-Video RL的82.57。

Conclusion: MeanFlowNFT成功将RL对齐引入MeanFlow生成器，具有严格策略改进保证，且能以极少采样步骤超越多步RL调优的扩散模型，展示了在效率与质量之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).

</details>


### [92] [Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15278)
*Zezhong Qian,Xiaowei Chi,Chak-Wing Mak,Tianze Zhou,Ruibin Yuan,Yuhan Rui,Hengzhe Sun,Zhuoqun Wu,Yuming Li,Siyuan Qian,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HDR是一种分层去噪框架，将视频潜在变量组织成树状层次结构，实现从粗到细的多步推理，在推理成功率（从34.22提升至60.29）和推理速度（比双向扩散快54.2倍）上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式自回归扩散模型缺乏多步推理能力，而双向扩散虽然能全局修正但推理成本高，两者均难以在复杂推理任务中实现逻辑一致性和低延迟流式生成。

Method: 提出分层去噪框架HDR，将视频潜在变量组织为树状层次结构：粗去噪层保留不确定假设用于全局规划，细去噪层逐步精化为具体视觉状态；引入稀疏分层注意力模式（SHAP）降低时序注意力成本。

Result: 在六项多步推理任务中，HDR将成功率从34.22提升至60.29（相对提升76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56；推理延迟0.70秒/潜在变量，比双向扩散快54.2倍；仅用2%训练数据即可保留82.9%的完整数据性能。

Conclusion: HDR在保持低延迟流式生成的同时，显著提升了视频模型的多步推理一致性和效率，并在真实机器人实验中展现了物理交互与世界建模的潜力。

Abstract: Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15202)
*Hoang-Loc Cao,Van Pham,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Phuc Ho,Veronica Whitford,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自演进、专家在环的MDD标注框架，结合LLM辅助标注与专家验证，生成符合DSM-5-TR标准、可解释、可审计的抑郁数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症数据集标注质量差，缺乏结构化证据、症状级理由及与DSM-5-TR标准的可追溯对齐，限制了透明度和模型可解释性。

Method: 三阶段框架：候选证据选择→标准级DSM-5-TR分析→案例级综合（生成诊断与严重程度标签）；双记忆架构（示例记忆+反思记忆）内化专家反馈，持续迭代改进。

Result: 初步专家评审实验中，该方法提升了标注一致性和可解释性，减少了人工修订工作量。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效构建可解释、符合DSM-5-TR标准的MDD数据集，但多轮反馈循环的评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.

</details>


### [94] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于自主无人机蜂群搜索救援的三级分层学习架构，整合了三种不同的学习机制（Hebbian神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习+图神经网络+行为树、模型无关元学习+BDI推理+数字孪生），通过22个架构契约提供六类形式化保证，并引入群体元认知作为组合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习方法存在根本性局限，单一学习范式无法有效应对搜救任务中不同层次（个体适应、战术协调、战略决策）的差异化需求。

Method: 三级分层架构：1) 反射层：Hebbian神经可塑性实现个体自适应；2) 技能层：多智能体强化学习+图神经网络+行为树实现战术协调；3) 推理层：模型无关元学习+BDI推理+数字孪生实现战略决策。通过22个架构契约形式化保证六类性质（安全性、预算正确性、最优性、活性、无饥饿、层间一致性）。引入群体元认知作为组合性质，以及五类搜救任务进度函数。

Result: 主集成定理证明当所有契约满足时，混合神经符号系统保留全部六类保证。动态学习场景下新增5个契约，额外提供认知韧性、优雅降级和单调元改进三类保证。该架构解决了现有分层RL方法的五个根本性局限。

Conclusion: 该架构通过生物启发式三级学习机制与形式化契约相结合，为无人机蜂群搜救任务提供了一种具有可验证保证的混合神经符号方法，并展示了群体元认知这一新兴组合性质。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [95] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HG-RAG是一种通过层次知识图谱进行图遍历来增强RAG系统的框架，在层次、关系和多跳推理任务上优于传统平面检索方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统从平面文档库检索上下文，在处理需要层次或关系推理的结构化知识查询时效果不佳。

Method: 从查询中解析命名实体锚点，然后通过父节点向上扩展、关系邻居横向扩展、子节点向下扩展上下文，形成层次化知识图谱的图遍历检索管道。

Result: 在三种规模（18-800节点）和四种查询类型上，HG-RAG在层次、关系和多跳推理任务上一致优于平面密集检索基线，同时减少了幻觉并保持了局部连贯性。

Conclusion: 层次化图遍历检索策略能有效提升RAG系统在结构化知识推理场景下的表现，是传统平面检索的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [96] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IMEX的交互式模型解释方法，通过静态相关力（PCS）和交互相关力（PCI）两种互补指标，能够识别对预测目标贡献最大的特征及其交互作用，并在合成数据集上验证了PCS组件优于INVASE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型缺乏透明性，使得即使预测准确也难以解释和验证其内部机制。在关键应用场景中，仅靠预测精度作为验证指标是不够的，模型决策背后的原因必须得到合理解释。

Method: IMEX方法基于两个互补指标：静态相关力（PCS）量化单个特征的贡献，交互相关力（PCI）捕捉特征间的非加性效应。该方法不限制高阶交互分析，可研究基数大于2的特征子集，从而构建预测的可解释性图谱。

Result: 通过在三个已知结构的合成数据集上将PCS组件与INVASE方法进行实验对比，结果表明IMEX能够在输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系的情况下，有效恢复相关特征级结构。

Conclusion: IMEX方法能够有效解释黑盒模型的预测机制，不仅识别重要特征，还能探索与潜在影响机制一致的特征交互模式，为可解释预测建模提供了一种可行的方法论方向。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [97] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RegNetAgents是一个基于AI的多智能体框架，用于在异构基因调控网络中识别受查询驱动的调控候选因子。该系统整合了TCGA肿瘤网络和GRELmLN单细胞网络，通过双重网络分类、OncoKB癌症基因筛选和MoA分配，有效识别癌症调控候选因子。在乳腺癌和结直肠癌的验证中，该框架显著富集了OncoKB注释的癌症基因，并支持从候选识别到生物学假设生成的全流程解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏统一分析不同来源（如bulk肿瘤和单细胞）基因调控网络的工具，难以系统性地进行跨网络的调控候选因子识别和比较。

Method: 采用多智能体LangGraph DAG工作流，整合TCGA和GRELmLN的ARACNe网络，进行双重网络分类、OncoKB癌症基因过滤、MoA分配，并基于跨网络证据一致性（Both、TCGA-only、GRELmLN-only）对候选因子排序。

Result: 在11个BRCA和12个COAD焦点基因中，TCGA来源候选因子显著富集（BRCA: Z=6.69, COAD: Z=6.95），GRELmLN来源也显著富集（BRCA: Z=5.51, COAD: Z=7.06），所有p<0.0001，且管家基因对照组无富集。

Conclusion: RegNetAgents为癌症基因组学中的跨网络调控候选因子识别建立了可解释的AI框架，支持从候选识别到生物学假设生成的端到端分析。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [98] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对话式地点识别（DlgPR），将传统静态一次检索的地理定位转变为交互式对话推理过程，并构建了首个大规模对话基准DlgQuest-Cities，实验表明该方法显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导的地理定位方法采用静态一次性检索范式，无法处理真实场景中自然语言描述的歧义性和信息不完整性。

Method: 提出DlgPR范式，包含DlgQuest-Cities对话基准和统一推理框架：交叉模态多级检索器+智能提问器DQ-pilot，采用课程学习（DQ-cities-20k监督微调→DQ-cities-10k GRPO强化学习），结合DDI和PRG两种对齐指标。

Result: 基于推理的对话式方法显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 对话式推理检索是解决地理定位中语言歧义性的有效范式，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [99] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-T1D方法，将强化学习与大语言模型结合，构建透明可解释的胰岛素泵控制器，在FDA批准的模拟器上实现73.5%血糖达标率，同时具备严格安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习驱动的闭环胰岛素泵系统虽然有效，但其"黑箱"特性导致患者和医生难以信任。本文旨在通过引入大语言模型的可解释性，解决这一信任障碍。

Method: 训练专家级强化学习系统，然后将其实例知识蒸馏到微调的LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B模型中，使大语言模型既能控制胰岛素输注，又能用自然语言解释决策逻辑。

Result: 在FDA批准的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器上测试，LLM控制器实现了73.5%的时间在血糖目标范围内（Time in Range），超越了原始强化学习系统的性能，并通过了严格的形式化安全验证，消除了幻觉问题。

Conclusion: LLM-T1D成功融合了强化学习的精确控制能力与大语言模型的人类可理解推理能力，为1型糖尿病的智能治疗提供了更透明、更可靠的解决方案，有望增强患者和医生的信任度。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [100] [Human AI Construction of Bayesian Networks for Operational Decision Support -- A Virtual Survey Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14141)
*Kumar Rahul,Shovan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出使用大语言模型构建贝叶斯信念网络的新方法，通过AI智能体面板结合专家判断与数据驱动学习，在替代医疗场景中验证发现主观规范比自我效能对患者就医意愿影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决贝叶斯信念网络构建中专家判断与数据驱动学习之间的鸿沟，现有方法要么依赖专家经验要么依赖大数据集，两者难以兼顾。

Method: 提出六步BBN框架：使用LLM创建具有特定角色和背景的AI智能体面板来估计概率，再通过截尾均值规则去除噪声。

Result: 自我效能虽是重要因素但实际因果影响较小，主观规范对患者就医意愿的作用更强，同时提升自信和社区规范是最有效策略。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的AI智能体方法能有效桥接专家判断与数据驱动学习，为BBN构建提供新途径。

Abstract: Bayesian Belief Networks (BBNs) are powerful tools for decision-making under uncertainty. However, building their structures and estimating parameters are difficult. Currently, researchers must choose between relying on expert judgement or using large datasets to learn the structure and parameters of the network. We propose a new methodology using Large Language Models to bridge the gap between expert opinion and data-driven learning. This approach uses a panel of AI agents to estimate probabilities based on specific personas and context. We then apply a trimmed-mean rule to remove noise from these responses. We develop a six step BBN framework and illustrate it to model customer intention to consult a doctor in an alternative healthcare system. The model reveals that while self efficacy appears to be a major factor, its actual causal impact is small. In contrast, subjective norms have a much stronger effect in modelling customers' intention. The most effective strategy is to improve both confidence and community norms simultaneously.

</details>


### [101] [Capability from Access Structure, Not Scale: Lower Bounds and Pre-Registered Tests for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14144)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出能力收敛假说（CCH），指出在固定推理预算下，表征收敛并不等同于能力收敛。能力收敛需要特定的访问结构——兼具压缩O(1)状态通道和可扩展逐字索引通道的混合架构，才能跨越香农墙、视界墙和电路墙这三重资源壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柏拉图表征假说（PRH）认为不同网络随规模扩大会收敛到共享的现实表征，但作者质疑：表征收敛是否意味着能力也收敛？他们试图揭示表征收敛与能力收敛之间的根本性脱节。

Method: 提出能力收敛假说（CCH），以牛顿苹果问题作为见证任务，从信息论下界和计算复杂性理论推导出三重资源壁垒（香农墙、视界墙、电路墙），并通过预注册的小规模实验验证剪刀差效应。

Result: 预注册实验证实：64标量状态加上一层全局注意力后，精确检索误差从0.994骤降至0.000，状态跟踪分叉点落在注册边界上，显现出不可约的双通道解决方案。一项预测失败（方向反转）已如实报告。

Conclusion: 表征收敛由规模自由赋予，但能力收敛必须通过访问结构购买。能力在复合操作下呈现严格超可加性。领域层面的收敛趋势是经济学动机的猜想，而可访问完备性原理则基于信息论下界和预注册实验支撑。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that as models scale, representations of heterogeneous networks converge toward a shared model of reality. We propose its sequel and boundary, the Capability Convergence Hypothesis (CCH): under a fixed per-token inference budget, representational convergence does not entail capability convergence. Capability instead converges toward a class, the access-complete hybrid: any architecture holding both a compressive O(1)-state channel and a scalable verbatim-index channel. We anchor it on a witness task, the Newton's-apple problem in an infinite stream, and name three resource walls: a Shannon wall barring any o(Nb)-state architecture, a horizon wall barring any fixed window, and a circuit wall barring fixed-depth attention-only composition (conditional on TC0 != NC1). Under an explicit separability assumption a hybrid crosses all three by paying each wall's price, so capability is strictly super-additive under composition. We separate what we prove from what we conjecture: the access-completeness principle rests on information-theoretic lower bounds and pre-registered experiments, while the field-level convergence trend is an economics-motivated conjecture. We report the first pre-registered small-scale tests under criteria frozen before the data: the predicted scissors gap is measured (exact-retrieval error 0.994 vs. 0.000 once a 64-scalar state gains one global-attention layer), the state-tracking bifurcation lands at the registered boundary, and a conjunction witness shows an irreducibly two-channel solution; one prediction failed with its direction reversed and is reported as such. Representational convergence is given freely by scale; capability convergence must be purchased by access structure.

</details>


### [102] [ToolAnchor: Anchoring Counterfactual Context to Boost Agentic Tool-use Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14145)
*Weiting Liu,Jieyi Bi,Wanqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Yining Ma,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolAnchor框架，通过注入反事实锚定上下文来打破大语言模型智能体在工具集扩展时的行为惯性，实现无需从头训练的动态工具适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型智能体在固定工具集上表现优异，但当任务需要新工具时，智能体难以有效整合新工具，且从头重新训练通常不可行。

Method: ToolAnchor框架：利用教师模型假设反事实上下文，通过学生模型 rollout 验证，并将成功干预内化为智能体后训练信号，从而打破行为惯性。

Result: 在GAIA、BrowseComp和VDR-Bench等任务上，ToolAnchor在扩展工具集下持续展现出有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了静态后训练与动态适应之间的鸿沟，为可扩展的智能体强化学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents excel at long-horizon tasks, yet they are typically post-trained on fixed toolsets. When tasks demand new tools, these agents struggle to incorporate them effectively, and retraining from scratch is often impractical. We identify the core obstacle in such toolset expansion problem as behavioral inertia: the tendency of agents to fall back on familiar tools and established reasoning patterns despite having access to new ones. We demonstrate that injecting counterfactual anchor contexts at critical decision points can break this inertia, recovering failed trajectories by eliciting suppressed agent capabilities. To scale this insight, we propose ToolAnchor, a framework that uses teacher models to hypothesize these counterfactual contexts, verifies them via student rollouts, and internalizes the successful interventions through agentic post-training. Extensive evaluations across general AI assistant (GAIA), textual search (BrowseComp), and visual search (VDR-Bench) tasks demonstrate that ToolAnchor consistently exhibits competitive performance under expanded toolsets. Our work bridges the gap between static post-training and dynamic adaptation, charting a new path for scalable agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [103] [Enhancing Small Language Models Reasoning through Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14149)
*Dimitrios Kelesis,Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种神经符号代理框架，通过将小型语言模型（Gemma 3 1B/4B、Llama 3.2 3B）转变为使用两个专用工具（extract_facts和get_hint）的智能体，在CLUTRR亲属关系基准上提升其推理能力。实验发现，RGCN提供的提示带来1.5-2倍的性能提升，但系统受限于提取瓶颈和顺序推理脆弱性，且存在"分心效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型零样本推理能力强但成本高且环境负担重，小型语言模型虽可持续但复杂多跳逻辑推理易出错，因此需要增强SLM的推理能力。

Method: 将SLM转变为极简代理，使用两个专用工具调用：extract_facts（符号三元组提取）和get_hint（通过RGCN提供专家推理提示）。在Oracle（真实三元组）和Realistic（自提取知识）两种配置下评估。

Result: RGCN提示带来1.5-2倍性能提升，但受限于提取瓶颈和顺序推理脆弱性（早期提取错误在多跳链中累积）。存在"分心效应"：某些架构中噪声自生成事实即使有专家提示也会降低性能。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了低资源代理系统中符号基础化的挑战，为神经符号代理流水线中的迭代验证提供了路线图。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have set benchmarks for zero-shot reasoning, their deployment remains cost-prohibitive and environmentally taxing. Small Language Models (SLMs) offer a sustainable alternative, but prone to errors, on tasks requiring complex, multi-hop logical grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic agentic framework to enhance the reasoning capabilities of SLMs, specifically Gemma 3 (1B, 4B) and Llama 3.2 (3B), using the CLUTRR kinship benchmark. Our approach transforms the SLM into a minimalist agent utilizing two specialized tool calls: extract_facts for symbolic triplet extraction and get_hint for expert reasoning via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (RGCN). We evaluate these models across two configurations, both in an Oracle scenario with ground-truth triplets and a Realistic scenario relying on self-extracted knowledge. Our results reveal that while RGCN-derived hints provide a 1.5 - 2x performance gain over story-only baselines, the system is constrained by the extraction bottleneck and sequential deductive fragility, where early extraction errors compound over multi-hop chains. Furthermore, we identify a "distraction effect" in specific architectures where noisy, self-generated facts degrade performance despite the presence of expert hints. This work characterizes the challenges of symbolic grounding in low-resource agentic systems and provides a roadmap for iterative verification in neuro-symbolic agentic pipelines.

</details>


### [104] [Orchestrating Power Grid Studies with Multi-Agent AI and MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14158)
*Jérôme Picault,Clément Goubet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用Agentic AI和模型上下文协议（MCP）支持输电系统运营商（TSO）背景下的电网研究，重点是将大语言模型与数值仿真工具、结构化工作流及人类监督相结合。作者提出了工业需求，介绍了pypowsbyl-mcp接口，并讨论了人机协同、多智能体工作流及评估策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前电网研究依赖大量人工操作，仿真工具与AI系统之间缺乏标准化接口，导致工作效率低、可扩展性差。作者希望通过MCP协议桥接AI代理与电网仿真工具，实现更高效、可审计、可扩展的电网研究环境。

Method: 1. 识别工业级需求；2. 开发pypowsbyl-mcp接口，将仿真工具pypowsbyl的能力暴露给AI代理；3. 设计人机协同（Human-in-the-loop）和多智能体工作流；4. 提出结合技术指标和实践者反馈的评估策略。

Result: 成功构建了pypowsbyl-mcp原型接口，使AI代理能够通过标准化工具调用设置仿真、执行分析、检索结果，并与电力系统仿真器交互。

Conclusion: MCP-based工具集成是迈向更交互、可审计、可扩展电网研究环境的重要一步，为未来AI辅助电力系统分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [105] [MemoHarness: Agent Harnesses That Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14159)
*Yue Huang,Wenjie Wang,Han Bao,Yuchen Ma,Xiaonan Luo,Yi Nian,Haomin Zhuang,Zheyuan Liu,Yue Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoHarness是一种自适应智能体框架优化框架，通过将框架分解为六个可编辑控制维度，并利用双层经验库存储诊断和全局模式，实现了无需测试时标签、反馈或搜索的案例自适应优化。实验表明其优于固定框架，并具有选择性迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架（harness）设计对智能体行为影响显著，但大多数自动改进方法只优化提示、流水线等工作流等较窄的工件，且部署的智能体通常对所有案例使用单一的全局框架，缺乏适应性。

Method: 将框架分解为上下文管理、工具、编排、记忆、解码和输出处理六个可编辑维度；构建双层经验库（案例级诊断+全局模式）；通过检索经验实现每个测试案例的适应性调整，无需测试时标签、反馈或额外搜索。

Result: 在shell智能体、代码生成和分析推理三个基准上，MemoHarness优于固定框架，并展现了对未见套件和基座模型的选择性迁移能力；当检索的经验可缓存时，额外上下文开销可保持成本竞争力。

Conclusion: 执行经验是构建比单一静态配置更具适应性的智能体框架的实用基础，但统计鲁棒性和组件归因等更广泛的主张留待未来工作验证。

Abstract: An agent harness is the external control layer that turns a base LLM into an executable agent by managing context, tools, orchestration, memory, decoding, and output handling. While harness design strongly affects agent behavior, most automatic improvement methods optimize narrower artifacts such as prompts, pipelines, or workflows, and deployed agents usually reuse a single global harness for all cases. We introduce MemoHarness, an adaptive harness optimization framework that learns from its own executions. MemoHarness decomposes the harness into six editable control dimensions, stores per-case diagnoses and distilled global patterns in a dual-layer experience bank, and adapts the learned harness to each test case using retrieved experience without test-time labels, feedback, or additional search. In our evaluation across shell-agent, code-generation, and analytical-reasoning benchmarks, MemoHarness improves over the fixed harnesses we compare against and shows selective transfer to unseen suites and base models. Its additional context can also remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable. These results provide evidence that execution experience is a practical substrate for building agent harnesses that are more adaptive than a single static configuration, while leaving broader claims about statistical robustness and component attribution to future work.

</details>


### [106] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，用高过渡准确率（如100%）验证LLM合成的代码世界模型（CWM）是误导性的，因为模型可能在规划者搜索分布上几乎完全正确，但缺失的关键规则（<1%）会导致系统性的游戏失败。作者将此称为“验证-正确差距”，并给出了定量规律、实验证据和理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM合成的世界模型通常通过采样轨迹的高过渡准确率来验证。作者认为这种验证标准对于规划任务是不充分的，因为模型即使在采样上完美也可能在关键动态上出错，导致规划失败。

Method: 通过端到端实验设计，让LLM（GPT-5.x系列）合成游戏规则作为可执行代码（CWM），然后用经典规划器进行搜索。对比采样验证准确率与真实游戏表现，并定量分析失败规律。同时扩展到不完全信息博弈中的信念推理函数，给出理论覆盖界。

Result: LLM合成的CWM可以在采样测试中达到100%过渡准确率、≥98%状态准确率，但在实际游戏中系统性失败。缺失关键规则（<1%错误）导致游戏失败，代价为0.091。作者给出了定量公式 danger = play_cost × (1 - rarity)^N。增加数据和模型规模也无法修复此问题。在不完全信息博弈中也存在类似机制。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型的充分性应通过在搜索分布上的表现或直接通过游戏表现来衡量，而非采样过渡预测准确率。

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning.
  We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples). (4) The same mechanism recurs on the belief-inference function of imperfect-information CWMs: we prove a coverage bound (a size-$N$ gate is identifying when $N\gtrsim b^{d_{\max}}$), explaining why shallow games such as Kuhn poker show no gap, and hand-construct Beacon, a verified-but-wrong inference function that passes the gate yet loses every game.
  These results suggest adequacy for planning-oriented world models should be measured on the search distribution or by play directly, not by prediction accuracy on sampled transitions.

</details>


### [107] [ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178)
*Yutong He,Daibo Li,Guohong Li,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang,Can Ren,Zekun Zhang,Yifan Liu,Shuchen Zhu,Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Ming Sun,Shu Li,Chenyi Li,Jiang Hu,Kun Yuan,Zaiwen Wen,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasFlow 是一个端到端的自主智能体系统，专为以推理为中心的科学发现设计。它采用人类专家作为首席研究员、智能体作为研究生的协作范式，通过内部验证循环和自动化知识检索与自我改进机制，能够从少量提示自动生成包含严谨理论和实证内容的完整研究论文，在LLM评估中达到了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究系统主要聚焦于经验驱动领域和定量基准，忽视了理论驱动的科学发现，特别是在需要严格证明和领域知识综合的数学基础学科中。关键挑战包括：理论推理的大规模验证困难、自主前沿探索的推理能力不足、以及文献中程序性启发知识的稀缺。

Method: ReasFlow 包含两大核心机制：(i) 内部验证循环——在人工检查前审计逻辑一致性并纠正基本错误；(ii) 自动化知识检索与自我改进机制——主动获取声明性事实和被忽视的程序性启发知识。系统统一了文献综合、算法设计、定理证明、实验和手稿准备等完整流程。

Result: 从少量提示自主生成五篇完整研究论文，包含严谨的理论和实证内容。在基于LLM的评审标准下，ReasFlow 在所有开源基线中持续获得最高评估分数。

Conclusion: ReasFlow 有效填补了理论驱动型科学发现的自动化空白，为AI辅助理论研究提供了可用的协作平台。系统已通过 ReasLab 平台公开访问。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models have fueled autonomous AI agents capable of tackling complex scientific tasks, yet existing automated research systems remain predominantly focused on empirically driven domains with quantitative benchmarks, leaving theory-driven discovery, particularly in mathematically grounded disciplines requiring rigorous proofs and synthesis of domain knowledge, largely underexplored. Key challenges include the difficulty of verifying theoretical reasoning at scale, insufficient reasoning ability for autonomous frontier exploration, and a scarcity of procedural heuristics in the literature. We introduce ReasFlow, an end-to-end autonomous agent system for reasoning-centric scientific discovery that operationalizes a collaborative paradigm where the human expert acts as Principal Investigator while the agent executes rigorous derivations as a capable graduate student. ReasFlow incorporates (i) a robust internal verification loop that audits logical coherence and corrects fundamental errors prior to human inspection, and (ii) an automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism that proactively surfaces both declarative facts and overlooked procedural heuristics, substantially reducing expert intervention. The system unifies literature synthesis, algorithm design, theorem proving, experimentation, and manuscript preparation in a single system. Deployed to autonomously generate five complete research papers with rigorous theoretical and empirical content from minimal prompts, ReasFlow consistently achieves the highest evaluation scores among state-of-the-art open-access baselines under a curated LLM-based review rubric. ReasFlow is publicly accessible via the ReasLab platform, providing a collaborative workspace for AI-assisted theoretical research. Github repo: https://github.com/ReasLab/ReasFlow.git.

</details>


### [108] [RxBrain: Embodied Cognition Foundation Model with Joint Language-Visual Reasoning and Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14187)
*Haotian Liang,Mingkang Chen,Yufei Huang,Yuchun Guo,Xiaomeng Zhu,Xiangli Shi,Kaixuan Wang,Yunxuan Mao,Weijie Zhou,Ling Chen,Shirong Zeng,Yueyu Long,Yuchen Si,Yajuan Zhu,Xingyu Zhou,Minghui Wang,Wanjia He,Xin Yang,Lingzhu Xiang,Zhiqing Liu,Bohan Ma,Xiran Huang,Tianshuo Yang,Zhiheng Liu,Xuantang Xiong,Zisheng Lu,Ping Luo,Yao Mu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hy-Embodied-RxBrain是一个具身认知基础模型，通过统一的多模态Mixture-of-Transformers架构，将语言推理与视觉想象结合在单一规划序列中，实现任务分解、状态预测和子目标规划，并在机器人动作生成中展现出良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型要么侧重场景理解和文本决策（视觉-语言模型），要么主要预测未来视觉状态（生成世界模型），缺乏将高层任务推理与物理状态实现相连接的具身认知能力。作者希望构建一个能够联合语言推理与视觉想象的统一模型。

Method: 1) 提出统一的多模态Mixture-of-Transformers架构，支持语言、图像和视频的理解与生成；2) 构建自动流水线，将具身视频转换为文本-视觉联合规划监督信号；3) 引入RxBrain-Bench评估框架。

Result: 模型保持了具身理解和生成能力，生成了耦合文本推理、世界状态预测和联合子目标规划的规划方案。扩展到连续机器人动作生成时，无需大规模动作数据预训练即展现出有前景的实际机器人性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作为构建具身认知基础模型迈出了初步一步，证明了将语言抽象结构与视觉想象结合在统一规划序列中的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied cognition requires agents to connect high-level task reasoning with the physical states to be achieved. We introduce Hy-Embodied-RxBrain, an embodied cognition foundation model with joint language-visual reasoning and imagination. Unlike vision-language models that emphasize scene understanding and textual decision making, or generative world models that mainly predict future visual states, RxBrain represents embodied plans in a single planning sequence where language and visual imagination play complementary roles. Language provides the abstract structure of a plan, including task decomposition, planning primitives, constraints, temporal order, and decision logic, while visual imagination grounds this structure through world state prediction and joint subgoal planning, associating each planning step with intermediate and final physical states. RxBrain adopts a unified multimodal Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports language, image, and video understanding and generation within one model. To train this capability, we build an automatic pipeline that converts embodied videos into joint text-visual planning supervision by decomposing videos into planning steps and aligning them with visual state transitions. We further introduce RxBrain-Bench to evaluate whether models can represent embodied plans through joint textual and visual components rather than separate understanding or generation. Experiments show that RxBrain maintains embodied understanding and generation abilities, and produces plans with coupled textual reasoning, world state prediction, and joint subgoal planning. We also extend RxBrain to continuous robot action generation, where it shows promising real-robot performance without large-scale action-data pretraining. These results provide an initial step toward foundation models for embodied cognition.

</details>


### [109] [How Artificial Intelligence LLM Engines Shape the Global Conflict Information Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14197)
*Jason Miklian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了五个AI问答引擎在28个冲突上的5,460个回答，发现记录越稀少的冲突，AI越容易产生幻觉、误归因和错误计数。这种"薄记录"不仅导致错误，还容易受到生成引擎优化（GEO）的操纵。作者分析了AI引用的1,048个网站，发现GEO源优化已在发生，国家相关数字渗透虽尚处初期但增长迅速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI问答引擎在回答和平与冲突相关问题时的错误模式，以及这些错误如何反映和影响全球冲突信息环境的变化。

Method: 向五个主流AI问答引擎提出关于28个冲突的一系列问题，对5,460个回答进行基于文档证据的评分；同时分析了AI引用的1,048个网站以评估GEO影响。

Result: 冲突相关记录越稀薄，AI引擎越容易产生幻觉、误归因和错误计数；薄记录容易受到GEO操纵；GEO源优化已在发生，国家相关的数字渗透虽处早期但增长迅速。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了GEO信息战对学术研究的意义，政策上应回归AI无法替代的深度本地监测和翻译研究，并讨论了未来研究机遇与挑战。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) answer engines now field a growing share of the questions that analysts, scholars, and the public ask about issues of peace and conflict. Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to hallucinate under certain conditions, but do these errors have discernible patterns when they are asked about conflicts, and if so what can that teach us about the changing global conflict information environment? To answer, we first asked a battery of questions about 28 conflicts to five leading answer engines and scored their 5,460 answers against documented evidence. We found that the thinner the retrievable record around a given conflict, the more the engines invent, misattribute, and miscount. Thin records don't just encourage hallucination, but create structural exposure to mis- and disinformation, because they are the easiest records to warp through Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to bias engine responses.
